- **dsh-spec-agent-experience**: Use when writing or changing a prompt — a system prompt, a tool definition (name, description, parameter schema, or its system-prompt section), a skill's instructions, context-loading instructions, or the steps of a multi-step workflow — to make the prompt's information discoverable and use context efficiently. 用于编写或修改提示词——system prompt、工具定义（名称、描述、参数 schema 或 system-prompt 段落）、技能指令、上下文加载指令或多步工作流的步骤——使提示词的信息可发现并高效使用上下文。 ([sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec](https://github.com/sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/dsh-spec-agent-experience) · 2 copies)

- **dsh-spec-code-review**: Use when reviewing a proposed software change — a pull request, merge request, patch set, diff, or working-tree change — to orient the reviewer to the project's declared standards (standing instructions, design rules, prose standards, decision records, quality gates) and to the review-specific checks that code alone cannot show. 用于审查软件项目中的 pull request、merge request、patch set、diff 或工作树变更：对照项目自己声明的标准（常驻指令、设计规则、文档与行文标准、决策记录、测试策略与质量门禁），并执行仅看代码无法覆盖的审查检查。 ([sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec](https://github.com/sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/dsh-spec-code-review) · 2 copies)

Not for transient chat text or code correctness. 用于编写、评审、恢复、修剪或审计持久性文本——在写作会话结束后仍会被阅读、后续读者依赖的文字——不论项目把它放在哪里：文档、注释、记录、指令、提示词、诊断信息或可见文案。涵盖判断何处需要文字、删除推理过程/重复/装饰而保全完整命题，以及审计或修复思维链泄漏。不用于即时对话文字或代码正确性。 ([sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec](https://github.com/sunandsunshine-tech/dsh-spec/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/dsh-spec-prose-standard) · 2 copies)
